Read source registrations.\n    try:\n        source = json.loads(source_hooks_json.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:\n        return (\n            False,\n            f\"❌ Source hooks.json malformed at {source_hooks_json}: {exc}\",\n            None,\n        )\n    source_hooks = source.get(\"hooks\", {})\n    if not isinstance(source_hooks, dict):\n        return (\n            False,\n            f\"❌ Source hooks.json has non-object 'hooks' key: {type(source_hooks).__name__}\",\n            None,\n        )\n\n    # Ensure target dir exists.\n    try:\n        target_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    except OSError as exc:\n        return (\n            False,\n            f\"❌ Could not create target dir {target_path.parent}: {exc}\",\n            None,\n        )\n\n    backup_path: Path | None = None\n\n    if target_path.exists():\n        # Backup BEFORE any write — preserves user's pre-install state.\n        backup_path = _backup_path(target_path)\n        try:\n            shutil.copy2(target_path, backup_path)\n        except OSError as exc:\n            return (\n                False,\n                f\"❌ Could not back up existing settings.json to {backup_path}: {exc}\",\n                None,\n            )\n\n        try:\n            existing = json.loads(target_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:\n            return (\n                False,\n                (\n                    f\"❌ Target settings.json is malformed; refusing to write. \"\n                    f\"Backup at {backup_path}. Parse error: {exc}\"\n                ),\n                backup_path,\n            )\n        if not isinstance(existing, dict):\n            return (\n                False,\n                (\n                    f\"❌ Target settings.json is not a JSON object; refusing to write. \"\n                    f\"Backup at {backup_path}.\"\n                ),\n                backup_path,\n            )\n    else:\n        existing = {}\n\n    existing_hooks = existing.get(\"hooks\") or {}\n    if not isinstance(existing_hooks, dict):\n        return (\n            False,\n            f\"❌ Target settings.json 'hooks' key has non-object type \"\n            f\"({type(existing_hooks).__name__}); refusing to write.\",\n            backup_path,\n        )\n\n    added = 0\n    skipped_collision = 0\n    replaced = 0\n\n    for event, registrations in source_hooks.items():\n        if not isinstance(registrations, list):\n            continue\n        event_list = existing_hooks.setdefault(event, [])\n        if not isinstance(event_list, list):\n            return (\n                False,\n                (\n                    f\"❌ Target settings.json hooks.{event} is not a list \"\n                    f\"({type(event_list).__name__}); refusing to write.\"\n                ),\n                backup_path,\n            )\n\n        # Snapshot the matchers that existed in the target BEFORE this merge.\n        # Collisions are user-vs-source, not source-vs-source: two of our own\n        # registrations with the same matcher (e.g., the SessionStart pair —\n        # session-init.sh + freshness-session-start.sh, both implicit/explicit\n        # `*` matcher) must BOTH land.\n        original_target_matchers = {\n            r.get(\"matcher\", \"*\") for r in event_list if isinstance(r, dict)\n        }\n        # Also track exact (matcher, command-list) tuples that already exist\n        # so a true duplicate (same matcher + same exact inner commands) is\n        # always treated as a collision regardless of source-vs-source distinction.\n        original_target_signatures = {\n            _registration_signature(r) for r in event_list if isinstance(r, dict)\n        }\n\n        for new_reg in registrations:\n            if not isinstance(new_reg, dict):\n                continue\n            new_matcher = new_reg.get(\"matcher\", \"*\")\n            new_signature = _registration_signature(new_reg)\n\n            # Exact-duplicate check: same matcher AND same inner commands →\n            # always a collision (no work to do).\n            if new_signature in original_target_signatures:\n                if force:\n                    # Find and replace the first matching original.\n                    for idx, existing_reg in enumerate(event_list):\n                        if (\n                            isinstance(existing_reg, dict)\n                            and _registration_signature(existing_reg) == new_signature\n                        ):\n                            event_list[idx] = new_reg\n                            replaced += 1\n                            break\n                else:\n                    skipped_collision += 1\n                continue\n\n            # Matcher-only collision against ORIGINAL target (user-vs-source):\n            # only counts when the user had this matcher pre-merge.\n            if new_matcher in original_target_matchers:\n                # Find the original registration with this matcher\n                collision_idx = None\n                for idx, existing_reg in enumerate(event_list):\n                    if (\n                        isinstance(existing_reg, dict)\n                        and existing_reg.get(\"matcher\", \"*\") == new_matcher\n                        and _registration_signature(existing_reg)\n                        in original_target_signatures\n                    ):\n                        collision_idx = idx\n                        break\n\n                if collision_idx is not None:\n                    if force:\n                        event_list[collision_idx] = new_reg\n                        replaced += 1\n                    else:\n                        skipped_collision += 1\n                    continue\n\n            # No collision with original target → append (even if it shares a\n            # matcher with a sibling we already added from this same source list).\n            event_list.append(new_reg)\n            added += 1\n\n    existing[\"hooks\"] = existing_hooks\n\n    # Atomic write: tempfile in same dir + os.replace.\n    try:\n        _atomic_write_json(target_path, existing)\n    except OSError as exc:\n        return (\n            False,\n            f\"❌ Atomic write to {target_path} failed: {exc}\",\n            backup_path,\n        )\n\n    summary_parts = [f\"📋 settings.json merge: events={len(source_hooks)}\"]\n    summary_parts.append(f\"added={added}\")\n    if replaced:\n        summary_parts.append(f\"replaced={replaced}\")\n    if skipped_collision:\n        summary_parts.append(\n            f\"skipped-collision={skipped_collision} (use --force to replace)\"\n        )\n    return True, \" \".join(summary_parts), backup_path\n\n\ndef _registration_signature(reg: dict) -> tuple:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build a hashable signature for a hook registration: (matcher, tuple-of-commands).\n    Used to detect exact duplicates regardless of object identity.\n    \"\"\"\n    matcher = reg.get(\"matcher\", \"*\")\n    inner = reg.get(\"hooks\", [])\n    if not isinstance(inner, list):\n        return (matcher, ())\n    commands = tuple(h.get(\"command\", \"\") for h in inner if isinstance(h, dict))\n    return (matcher, commands)\n\n\ndef _atomic_write_json(target: Path, payload: dict) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Write `payload` to `target` atomically via tempfile + os.replace.\"\"\"\n    parent = target.parent\n    # Use a deterministic-prefix temp name in the same directory so os.replace\n    # is a same-filesystem operation (atomic on POSIX).\n    tmp = parent / f\".{target.name}.tmp.{os.getpid()}\"\n    try:\n        tmp.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + \"\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n        os.replace(tmp, target)\n    finally:\n        # If os.replace succeeded, tmp no longer exists (rename). If it\n        # failed, clean up.\n        if tmp.exists():\n            try:\n                tmp.unlink()\n            except OSError:\n                pass\n\n\ndef _backup_path(target: Path) -> Path:\n    # ISO-8601 with no colons (colons are problematic on some filesystems).\n    stamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ\")\n    return target.with_suffix(target.suffix + f\".bak.{stamp}\")\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Source-path resolution\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef _get_hooks_source() -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Locate the canonical hooks.json. Same convention as install_core.\"\"\"\n    package_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent\n    package_hooks = package_root / \"hooks\" / \"hooks.json\"\n    return package_hooks\n\n\ndef _get_hooks_scripts_source() -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Locate src/superclaude/hooks/scripts/ in the package or checkout.\"\"\"\n    package_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent\n    return package_root / \"hooks\" / \"scripts\"\n\n\ndef _get_hooks_source_root() -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Locate src/superclaude/hooks/ (parent of scripts/ and home of seed data files).\"\"\"\n    package_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent\n    return package_root / \"hooks\"\n\n\ndef _deploy_seed_files(\n    *, hooks_source_root: Path, dest_root: Path\n) -> Tuple[list[str], list[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Deploy seed data files from src/superclaude/hooks/<src_name> to dest_root/<dest_name>.\n\n    Skip-if-exists semantics: never overwrites an existing destination, even when\n    install_hooks(force=True). This preserves user customizations to data files\n    like auggie-projects.txt across re-installs.\n\n    Best-effort on OSError: a missed seed deploy must not fail the broader install.\n    Hooks degrade gracefully when seed data is absent.\n\n    Returns (copied, skipped) lists of destination names.\n    \"\"\"\n    copied: list[str] = []\n    skipped: list[str] = []\n    for src_name, dest_name in _SEED_FILES:\n        src = hooks_source_root / src_name\n        dest = dest_root / dest_name\n        if not src.exists():\n            continue\n        if dest.exists():\n            skipped.append(dest_name)\n            continue\n        try:\n            shutil.copy2(src, dest)\n            copied.append(dest_name)\n        except OSError:\n            # Intentionally silent — telemetry path is per-hook, not per-install.\n            pass\n    return copied, skipped\n\n\ndef _get_legacy_scripts_source() -> Path | None:\n    \"\"\"Locate src/superclaude/scripts/ for the pre-existing session-init.sh.\"\"\"\n    package_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent\n    candidate = package_root / \"scripts\"\n    if candidate.exists():\n        return candidate\n    return None\n\n\n_SESSION_ID_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$\")\n\n\ndef validate_session_id(session_id: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"\n    Validate a session_id is filesystem-safe (used by hook scripts to interpolate\n    into ~/.claude/state/<bucket>/<session_id>.txt paths).\n\n    This is a defense-in-depth helper exposed for hook-author convenience; the\n    hooks themselves do not currently sanitize. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Eu6ayD75ZMhFre9uGPmQJH","type":"tool_result","content":"100\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n101\tfrom pathlib import Path\n102\t\n103\tfrom .config import EvalConfig, ScratchRootViolation, resolve_scratch_root\n104\tfrom .loader import InvalidEvalId, validate_eval_id\n105\t\n106\t__all__ = [\n107\t    \"SETUP_FAILED_TAG_RELPATH\",\n108\t    \"HomeContainmentViolation\",\n109\t    \"HomeIsolation\",\n110\t    \"containment_guard\",\n111\t]\n112\t\n113\t\n114\t# NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup contract — see ``HomeIsolation.setup`` docstring and\n115\t# D-0033/spec.md. The tag is written under the per-eval HOME so that the\n116\t# directory becomes self-describing for downstream consumers (the orchestrator,\n117\t# the EvalRunner, post-mortem operators): a HOME that carries this relative\n118\t# path is one whose setup raised AFTER ``tempfile.mkdtemp`` materialized the\n119\t# directory and BEFORE ``setup`` returned successfully. The constant is\n120\t# exported so :class:`EvalRunner` (T03.x) and forensics tooling can locate the\n121\t# tag without re-deriving the path.\n122\tSETUP_FAILED_TAG_RELPATH = \".eval-meta/setup_failed\"\n123\t\n124\t\n125\tdef _write_setup_failed_tag(home_path: Path, exc: BaseException) -> None:\n126\t    \"\"\"Write the NFR-ISO2 ``setup_failed`` artifact tag under ``home_path``.\n127\t\n128\t    The tag distinguishes \"harness bug or environmental refusal during\n129\t    setup\" (status ERRORED) from \"eval ran and produced a failing\n130\t    assertion\" (status FAIL). The contents are split for grep-ability:\n131\t\n132\t    * Line 1 is the exception class name verbatim\n133\t      (``type(exc).__name__``), so a downstream EvalRunner can bucket\n134\t      the error with a single ``head -n1`` read.\n135\t    * The remainder is the formatted traceback for forensic inspection.\n136\t\n137\t    The helper is best-effort: ``setup`` catches and discards any\n138\t    exception this function raises so a write failure (e.g. the\n139\t    ``.eval-meta`` directory cannot be created because the per-eval HOME\n140\t    was made read-only by a future hardening step) never masks the\n141\t    original setup exception.\n142\t\n143\t    Args:\n144\t        home_path: The per-eval HOME ``mkdtemp``-ed by\n145\t            :meth:`HomeIsolation.setup`. The tag is materialized at\n146\t            ``home_path / SETUP_FAILED_TAG_RELPATH``.\n147\t        exc: The exception that interrupted ``setup``. Used only to\n148\t            populate the tag contents; this helper never re-raises it.\n149\t    \"\"\"\n150\t\n151\t    tag_path = home_path / SETUP_FAILED_TAG_RELPATH\n152\t    tag_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n153\t    # ``traceback.format_exception`` reads the active exception state when\n154\t    # the helper is invoked from inside an ``except`` clause. A separate copy\n   pipeline would drift; the COMP-014 reuse contract pins this to the\n   one installer.\n2. **Deploy ``hooks.json`` verbatim.** ``install_hooks`` merges hook\n   registrations *into* ``settings.json`` but never copies the source\n   ``hooks.json`` to the target HOME. The acceptance criteria for T02.14\n   require ``<home_path>/.claude/hooks.json`` to be byte-identical to\n   ``src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json`` so post-mortem inspection and the\n   FR-G* gate tests can SHA256-compare the source-of-truth registrations\n   against what the per-eval HOME shipped. The adapter performs this\n   second copy itself.\n\nDefense-in-depth: a pre-flight refusal asserts ``home_path`` is not the\nreal ``~/.claude/`` (resolves both sides; rejects any equality or\ndescendant relationship). This complements but does not replace the\n:class:`HomeIsolation` containment guard — the adapter MAY be invoked\ndirectly by tests, so the guard must live here too.\n\nIdempotency: re-invoking ``deploy_hooks_to`` against the same\n``home_path`` produces identical filesystem state. ``install_hooks``\nitself is idempotent (existing scripts are skipped without ``--force``;\nthe settings.json merge skips matcher collisions). The verbatim\n``hooks.json`` copy uses ``shutil.copy2`` which overwrites with the same\nbytes when the source is unchanged.\n\nError contract: any failure inside the adapter raises\n:class:`HookDeployFailed` with an :attr:`HookDeployFailed.error_tag`\nattribute the EvalRunner (T03.x) propagates into\n``EvalRunner.outcome.artifacts``. The tag is a short kebab-case\nidentifier the orchestrator buckets ERRORED evals by without parsing\nhuman-readable strings.\n\nCross-links\n===========\n\n* COMP-006 :class:`HomeIsolation` (T02.11 / D-0032) — supplies the\n  ``home_path`` argument.\n* NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup wrapper (T02.13 / D-0033) — invokes the\n  adapter from inside ``HomeIsolation.setup``'s post-mkdtemp block in\n  a future wiring task.\n* :func:`superclaude.cli.install_hooks.install_hooks` — the underlying\n  installer this adapter wraps.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom ..install_hooks import install_hooks\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"HookDeployFailed\",\n    \"deploy_hooks_to\",\n]\n\n\n# Relative paths under the per-eval HOME that the adapter writes to.\n_SETTINGS_RELPATH = (\".claude\", \"settings.json\")\n_HOOKS_JSON_RELPATH = (\".claude\", \"hooks.json\")\n\n\nclass HookDeployFailed(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised by :func:`deploy_hooks_to` when hook deployment cannot complete.\n\n    Carries a short kebab-case :attr:`error_tag` the orchestrator\n    (:class:`EvalRunner`, T03.x) routes into\n    ``EvalRunner.outcome.artifacts`` so failed evals can be bucketed\n    without parsing the human-readable message.\n\n    Attributes:\n        error_tag: Short identifier for the failure mode. One of:\n\n            * ``\"refused-real-home\"`` — ``home_path`` resolved to (or\n              under) the real ``~/.claude/``; defense-in-depth guard\n              fired BEFORE any filesystem write.\n            * ``\"install-hooks-failed\"`` — the underlying\n              :func:`install_hooks` returned ``success=False``. The\n              installer's message is preserved in :attr:`detail`.\n            * ``\"hooks-json-copy-failed\"`` — the verbatim\n              ``hooks.json`` copy raised :class:`OSError`. The OS\n              error message is preserved in :attr:`detail`.\n            * ``\"source-hooks-json-missing\"`` — the source\n              ``src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json`` is not on disk.\n              Should never happen in a working checkout; surfaced\n              explicitly to make missing-source bugs loud.\n\n        home_path: The per-eval HOME the adapter was asked to deploy to.\n        detail: Human-readable failure detail (installer message,\n            ``OSError`` rendering, etc.). Never contains the error_tag\n            itself so the two surfaces stay independent.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        *,\n        error_tag: str,\n        home_path: Path,\n        detail: str,\n    ) -> None:\n        self.error_tag = error_tag\n        self.home_path = home_path\n        self.detail = detail\n        super().__init__(\n            f\"COMP-014 hook deploy failed [{error_tag}] for home_path={home_path!s}: {detail}\"\n        )\n\n\ndef _real_user_claude_dir() -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Resolve the user's real ``~/.claude/`` directory.\n\n    Returns the resolved (symlinks collapsed) path. Used by the\n    defense-in-depth refusal so a per-eval ``home_path`` that is a\n    symlink to the real user HOME still fails the equality / descendant\n    check.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    return (Path.home() / \".claude\").resolve(strict=False)\n\n\ndef _refuse_real_home(home_path: Path) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Reject ``home_path`` if it resolves under the real ``~/.claude/``.\n\n    The adapter MUST NOT write under the user's real Claude Code\n    configuration even when the caller bypassed\n    :class:`HomeIsolation.setup` (e.g., a test constructs an arbitrary\n    path and calls :func:`deploy_hooks_to` directly). The check resolves\n    both sides so a symlink in either direction is caught.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    real = _real_user_claude_dir()\n    resolved = home_path.resolve(strict=False)\n    if resolved == real or resolved.is_relative_to(real):\n        raise HookDeployFailed(\n            error_tag=\"refused-real-home\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            detail=(\n                f\"home_path {home_path!s} resolves to {resolved!s}, which \"\n                f\"matches the real user Claude dir {real!s}; refusing to \"\n                f\"write hooks under the real ~/.claude/\"\n            ),\n        )\n\n\ndef _source_hooks_json() -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Locate the canonical ``src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json``.\n\n    Mirrors :func:`superclaude.cli.install_hooks._get_hooks_source` so\n    the adapter and the installer agree on which file is the source of\n    truth. Imported via the public ``install_hooks`` to avoid coupling\n    on a private helper that may move.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # ``hook_adapter.py`` lives at src/superclaude/cli/eval/hook_adapter.py.\n    # Two parents up is the package root (src/superclaude/).\n    package_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent\n    return package_root / \"hooks\" / \"hooks.json\"\n\n\ndef deploy_hooks_to(home_path: Path) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Deploy SuperClaude hooks into the per-eval HOME at ``home_path``.\n\n    Two filesystem effects, in this order:\n\n    1. :func:`install_hooks` is invoked with ``target_path`` resolved to\n       ``<home_path>/.claude/settings.json``. This copies the hook\n       scripts to ``<home_path>/.claude/hooks/`` and merges hook\n       registrations into a fresh ``<home_path>/.claude/settings.json``.\n    2. The source ``src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json`` is copied\n       verbatim to ``<home_path>/.claude/hooks.json`` (byte-identical\n       via :func:`shutil.copy2`).\n\n    Idempotent: re-invocation on the same ``home_path`` produces\n    identical filesystem state. The installer skips already-deployed\n    scripts and matcher-colliding registrations; the verbatim copy\n    overwrites with the same bytes when the source is unchanged.\n\n    Args:\n        home_path: Per-eval HOME directory created by\n            :class:`HomeIsolation.setup`. MUST NOT resolve to the real\n            ``~/.claude/`` directory — the pre-flight guard rejects\n            that case before any write happens.\n\n    Raises:\n        HookDeployFailed: Any of the failure modes documented on\n            :class:`HookDeployFailed.error_tag`. Each failure mode\n            carries enough detail (``home_path`` + ``detail``) for the\n            orchestrator to bucket the eval and surface the underlying\n            cause in operator-facing reports.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Step 0 — defense-in-depth refusal. Must run BEFORE any write so\n    # `install_hooks` never even materializes a directory under real\n    # `~/.claude/`.\n    _refuse_real_home(home_path)\n\n    source_hooks_json = _source_hooks_json()\n    if not source_hooks_json.exists():\n        raise HookDeployFailed(\n            error_tag=\"source-hooks-json-missing\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            detail=f\"source hooks.json not found at {source_hooks_json!s}\",\n        )\n\n    # Step 1 — delegate scripts + settings.json merge to install_hooks.\n    settings_path = home_path.joinpath(*_SETTINGS_RELPATH)\n    success, message = install_hooks(target_path=settings_path, force=False)\n    if not success:\n        raise HookDeployFailed(\n            error_tag=\"install-hooks-failed\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            detail=message,\n        )\n\n    # Step 2 — verbatim hooks.json copy. Required by the AC: the\n    # per-eval HOME must carry a SHA256-identical copy of the source\n    # registrations so post-mortem inspection / FR-G* gate tests can\n    # diff against the canonical file.\n    dest_hooks_json = home_path.joinpath(*_HOOKS_JSON_RELPATH)\n    try:\n        dest_hooks_json.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        shutil.copy2(source_hooks_json, dest_hooks_json)\n    except OSError as exc:\n        raise HookDeployFailed(\n            error_tag=\"hooks-json-copy-failed\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            detail=f\"verbatim hooks.json copy from {source_hooks_json!s} \"\n            f\"to {dest_hooks_json!s} failed: {exc}\",\n        ) from exc\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1180,"cache_read_input_tokens":120792,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1180,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":52,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available","speed":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9b082b2d-581e-4312-b64d-a1e7c7afbf63","uuid":"5d691e07-27d9-49f1-af1f-f867c519c0ff"}

The OS\n              error message is preserved in :attr:`detail`.\n            * ``\"source-hooks-json-missing\"`` — the source\n              ``src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json`` is not on disk.\n              Should never happen in a working checkout; surfaced\n              explicitly to make missing-source bugs loud.\n\n        home_path: The per-eval HOME the adapter was asked to deploy to.\n        detail: Human-readable failure detail (installer message,\n            ``OSError`` rendering, etc.). Never contains the error_tag\n            itself so the two surfaces stay independent.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        *,\n        error_tag: str,\n        home_path: Path,\n        detail: str,\n    ) -> None:\n        self.error_tag = error_tag\n        self.home_path = home_path\n        self.detail = detail\n        super().__init__(\n            f\"COMP-014 hook deploy failed [{error_tag}] for home_path={home_path!s}: {detail}\"\n        )\n\n\ndef _real_user_claude_dir() -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Resolve the user's real ``~/.claude/`` directory.\n\n    Returns the resolved (symlinks collapsed) path. Used by the\n    defense-in-depth refusal so a per-eval ``home_path`` that is a\n    symlink to the real user HOME still fails the equality / descendant\n    check.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    return (Path.home() / \".claude\").resolve(strict=False)\n\n\ndef _refuse_real_home(home_path: Path) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Reject ``home_path`` if it resolves under the real ``~/.claude/``.\n\n    The adapter MUST NOT write under the user's real Claude Code\n    configuration even when the caller bypassed\n    :class:`HomeIsolation.setup` (e.g., a test constructs an arbitrary\n    path and calls :func:`deploy_hooks_to` directly). The check resolves\n    both sides so a symlink in either direction is caught.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    real = _real_user_claude_dir()\n    resolved = home_path.resolve(strict=False)\n    if resolved == real or resolved.is_relative_to(real):\n        raise HookDeployFailed(\n            error_tag=\"refused-real-home\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            detail=(\n                f\"home_path {home_path!s} resolves to {resolved!s}, which \"\n                f\"matches the real user Claude dir {real!s}; refusing to \"\n                f\"write hooks under the real ~/.claude/\"\n            ),\n        )\n\n\ndef _source_hooks_json() -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Locate the canonical ``src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json``.\n\n    Mirrors :func:`superclaude.cli.install_hooks._get_hooks_source` so\n    the adapter and the installer agree on which file is the source of\n    truth. Imported via the public ``install_hooks`` to avoid coupling\n    on a private helper that may move.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # ``hook_adapter.py`` lives at src/superclaude/cli/eval/hook_adapter.py.\n    # Two parents up is the package root (src/superclaude/).\n    package_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent\n    return package_root / \"hooks\" / \"hooks.json\"\n\n\ndef deploy_hooks_to(home_path: Path) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Deploy SuperClaude hooks into the per-eval HOME at ``home_path``.\n\n    Two filesystem effects, in this order:\n\n    1. :func:`install_hooks` is invoked with ``target_path`` resolved to\n       ``<home_path>/.claude/settings.json``. This copies the hook\n       scripts to ``<home_path>/.claude/hooks/`` and merges hook\n       registrations into a fresh ``<home_path>/.claude/settings.json``.\n    2. The source ``src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json`` is copied\n       verbatim to ``<home_path>/.claude/hooks.json`` (byte-identical\n       via :func:`shutil.copy2`).\n\n    Idempotent: re-invocation on the same ``home_path`` produces\n    identical filesystem state. The installer skips already-deployed\n    scripts and matcher-colliding registrations; the verbatim copy\n    overwrites with the same bytes when the source is unchanged.\n\n    Args:\n        home_path: Per-eval HOME directory created by\n            :class:`HomeIsolation.setup`. MUST NOT resolve to the real\n            ``~/.claude/`` directory — the pre-flight guard rejects\n            that case before any write happens.\n\n    Raises:\n        HookDeployFailed: Any of the failure modes documented on\n            :class:`HookDeployFailed.error_tag`. Each failure mode\n            carries enough detail (``home_path`` + ``detail``) for the\n            orchestrator to bucket the eval and surface the underlying\n            cause in operator-facing reports.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Step 0 — defense-in-depth refusal. Must run BEFORE any write so\n    # `install_hooks` never even materializes a directory under real\n    # `~/.claude/`.\n    _refuse_real_home(home_path)\n\n    source_hooks_json = _source_hooks_json()\n    if not source_hooks_json.exists():\n        raise HookDeployFailed(\n            error_tag=\"source-hooks-json-missing\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            detail=f\"source hooks.json not found at {source_hooks_json!s}\",\n        )\n\n    # Step 1 — delegate scripts + settings.json merge to install_hooks.\n    settings_path = home_path.joinpath(*_SETTINGS_RELPATH)\n    success, message = install_hooks(target_path=settings_path, force=False)\n    if not success:\n        raise HookDeployFailed(\n            error_tag=\"install-hooks-failed\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            detail=message,\n        )\n\n    # Step 2 — verbatim hooks.json copy. Required by the AC: the\n    # per-eval HOME must carry a SHA256-identical copy of the source\n    # registrations so post-mortem inspection / FR-G* gate tests can\n    # diff against the canonical file.\n    dest_hooks_json = home_path.joinpath(*_HOOKS_JSON_RELPATH)\n    try:\n        dest_hooks_json.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        shutil.copy2(source_hooks_json, dest_hooks_json)\n    except OSError as exc:\n        raise HookDeployFailed(\n            error_tag=\"hooks-json-copy-failed\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            detail=f\"verbatim hooks.json copy from {source_hooks_json!s} \"\n            f\"to {dest_hooks_json!s} failed: {exc}\",\n        ) from exc\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

The error-tag surface is structured: each failure mode carries a\n   kebab-case ``error_tag`` the orchestrator can route into\n   ``EvalRunner.outcome.artifacts`` without parsing strings.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom unittest.mock import patch\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom superclaude.cli.eval import (\n    HookDeployFailed,\n    deploy_hooks_to,\n)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Helpers\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef _sha256_of(path: Path) -> str:\n    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n\n\ndef _source_hooks_json() -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Resolve src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json the same way the adapter does.\"\"\"\n\n    # tests/cli/eval/test_hook_adapter.py is at <repo>/tests/cli/eval/.\n    # The source file is at <repo>/src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json.\n    repo_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]\n    return repo_root / \"src\" / \"superclaude\" / \"hooks\" / \"hooks.json\"\n\n\ndef _snapshot_real_home_mtime() -> float | None:\n    \"\"\"Snapshot mtime of the real ``~/.claude/`` directory if it exists.\n\n    Returns ``None`` when the directory is absent (CI hosts without a\n    user Claude install). Callers compare against\n    :func:`_real_home_mtime_unchanged` to assert no writes leaked.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    real = Path.home() / \".claude\"\n    if not real.exists():\n        return None\n    return real.stat().st_mtime\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Fixtures\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef per_eval_home(tmp_path: Path) -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Per-test scratch directory mirroring the per-eval HOME shape.\"\"\"\n\n    home = tmp_path / \"eval-home\"\n    home.mkdir()\n    return home\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef real_home_mtime_guard() -> float | None:\n    \"\"\"Capture mtime of real ``~/.claude/`` at fixture entry.\"\"\"\n\n    return _snapshot_real_home_mtime()\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# AC1 — deploy_hooks_to exists with the documented signature\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef test_deploy_hooks_to_writes_settings_json_under_home_path(per_eval_home: Path) -> None:\n    \"\"\"``install_hooks`` is invoked with ``target_path`` under the per-eval HOME.\"\"\"\n\n    deploy_hooks_to(per_eval_home)\n\n    settings_path = per_eval_home / \".claude\" / \"settings.json\"\n    assert settings_path.exists(), \"settings.json must be created under per-eval HOME\"\n\n    payload = json.loads(settings_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    assert isinstance(payload, dict)\n    # The merge must have populated the hooks key with the source events.\n    assert \"hooks\" in payload\n    assert \"SessionStart\" in payload[\"hooks\"]\n\n\ndef test_deploy_hooks_to_copies_hook_scripts(per_eval_home: Path) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Hook scripts are deployed to ``<home>/.claude/hooks/``.\"\"\"\n\n    deploy_hooks_to(per_eval_home)\n\n    scripts_dir = per_eval_home / \".claude\" / \"hooks\"\n    assert scripts_dir.is_dir()\n    # Snapshot byte-content of the three artefacts the adapter materializes.\n    settings_first = (claude_dir / \"settings.json\").read_bytes()\n    hooks_json_first = (claude_dir / \"hooks.json\").read_bytes()\n    script_names_first = sorted(p.name for p in (claude_dir / \"hooks\").iterdir())\n\n    deploy_hooks_to(per_eval_home)\n\n    settings_second = (claude_dir / \"settings.json\").read_bytes()\n    hooks_json_second = (claude_dir / \"hooks.json\").read_bytes()\n    script_names_second = sorted(p.name for p in (claude_dir / \"hooks\").iterdir())\n\n    assert settings_first == settings_second\n    assert hooks_json_first == hooks_json_second\n    assert script_names_first == script_names_second\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# AC4 — never writes to real ~/.claude/\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef test_real_user_claude_dir_is_untouched(\n    per_eval_home: Path, real_home_mtime_guard: float | None\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Mtime of real ``~/.claude/`` is unchanged by a successful deploy.\"\"\"\n\n    deploy_hooks_to(per_eval_home)\n\n    post_mtime = _snapshot_real_home_mtime()\n    assert post_mtime == real_home_mtime_guard, (\n        \"deploy_hooks_to must never touch the real ~/.claude/ \"\n        f\"(mtime before={real_home_mtime_guard} after={post_mtime})\"\n    )\n\n\ndef test_refuses_when_home_path_is_real_user_claude_dir(per_eval_home: Path) -> None:\n    \"\"\"``HookDeployFailed`` with ``refused-real-home`` when ``home_path`` is real ~/.claude/.\"\"\"\n\n    real_claude = Path.home() / \".claude\"\n    # The defense-in-depth guard compares the resolved ``home_path`` against\n    # the resolved real Claude dir; passing the *parent* of that dir as\n    # ``home_path`` would cause `install_hooks` to land settings.json under\n    # the real `~/.claude/`. Assert refusal happens first.\n    with pytest.raises(HookDeployFailed) as exc_info:\n        deploy_hooks_to(real_claude.parent)  # i.e., $HOME itself\n    assert exc_info.value.error_tag == \"refused-real-home\"\n    assert exc_info.value.home_path == real_claude.parent\n\n\ndef test_refuses_when_home_path_is_descendant_of_real_user_claude_dir(\n    tmp_path: Path,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Refusal also fires when ``home_path`` lives under real ~/.claude/.\"\"\"\n\n    # Synthesize a fake ``Path.home()`` so the test never actually has to\n    # touch the operator's real $HOME.  \"\"\"When ``install_hooks`` returns ``(False, msg)`` the adapter raises with\n    ``error_tag='install-hooks-failed'`` and the installer message in ``detail``.\"\"\"\n\n    installer_msg = \"❌ pytest.raises(HookDeployFailed) as exc_info:\n            deploy_hooks_to(per_eval_home)\n    assert exc_info.value.error_tag == \"install-hooks-failed\"\n    assert installer_msg in exc_info.value.detail\n    assert exc_info.value.home_path == per_eval_home\n\n\ndef test_missing_source_hooks_json_raises_hook_deploy_failed(\n    per_eval_home: Path, tmp_path: Path\n) -> None:\n error in ``detail``.\"\"\"\n\n    # Let the install_hooks step succeed, then induce a failure inside the\n    # verbatim copy. This complements but does not replace the\n:class:`HomeIsolation` containment guard — the adapter MAY be invoked\ndirectly by tests, so the guard must live here too.\n\nIdempotency: re-invoking ``deploy_hooks_to`` against the same\n``home_path`` produces identical filesystem state. ``install_hooks``\nitself is idempotent (existing scripts are skipped without ``--force``;\nthe settings.json merge skips matcher collisions). The verbatim\n``hooks.json`` copy uses ``shutil.copy2`` which overwrites with the same\nbytes when the source is unchanged.\n\nError contract: any failure inside the adapter raises\n:class:`HookDeployFailed` with an :attr:`HookDeployFailed.error_tag`\nattribute the EvalRunner (T03.x) propagates into\n``EvalRunner.outcome.artifacts``. Tests targeting the\n    ``hooks-json-copy-failed`` error tag patch this function instead\n    of the shared ``shutil.copy2`` global, which would also break\n    :func:`install_hooks` (it copies hook scripts through the same\n    ``shutil.copy2`` API).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    shutil.copy2(src, dest)","originalFile":"\"\"\"COMP-014 install_hooks adapter for per-eval HOME (Task T02.14 / D-0034).\n\nThis module is the single integration point between the cliEval harness's\nper-eval ``HOME`` (materialized by :class:`HomeIsolation` in T02.11) and\nthe production hook installer at :mod:`superclaude.cli.install_hooks`.\nThe adapter has two jobs:\n\n1. **Reuse, do not duplicate.** Delegate the script-copy + settings.json\n   merge to the existing ``install_hooks`` so the per-eval hook surface\n   is exactly the production user-install surface. A separate copy\n   pipeline would drift; the COMP-014 reuse contract pins this to the\n   one installer.\n2. **Deploy ``hooks.json`` verbatim.** ``install_hooks`` merges hook\n   registrations *into* ``settings.json`` but never copies the source\n   ``hooks.json`` to the target HOME. The acceptance criteria for T02.14\n   require ``<home_path>/.claude/hooks.json`` to be byte-identical to\n   ``src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json`` so post-mortem inspection and the\n   FR-G* gate tests can SHA256-compare the source-of-truth registrations\n   against what the per-eval HOME shipped. The adapter performs this\n   second copy itself.\n\nDefense-in-depth: a pre-flight refusal asserts ``home_path`` is not the\nreal ``~/.claude/`` (resolves both sides; rejects any equality or\ndescendant relationship). This complements but does not replace the\n:class:`HomeIsolation` containment guard — the adapter MAY be invoked\ndirectly by tests, so the guard must live here too.\n\nIdempotency: re-invoking ``deploy_hooks_to`` against the same\n``home_path`` produces identical filesystem state. ``install_hooks``\nitself is idempotent (existing scripts are skipped without ``--force``;\nthe settings.json merge skips matcher collisions). The verbatim\n``hooks.json`` copy uses ``shutil.copy2`` which overwrites with the same\nbytes when the source is unchanged.\n\nError contract: any failure inside the adapter raises\n:class:`HookDeployFailed` with an :attr:`HookDeployFailed.error_tag`\nattribute the EvalRunner (T03.x) propagates into\n``EvalRunner.outcome.artifacts``. The tag is a short kebab-case\nidentifier the orchestrator buckets ERRORED evals by without parsing\nhuman-readable strings.\n\nCross-links\n===========\n\n* COMP-006 :class:`HomeIsolation` (T02.11 / D-0032) — supplies the\n  ``home_path`` argument.\n* NFR-ISO2 atomic-setup wrapper (T02.13 / D-0033) — invokes the\n  adapter from inside ``HomeIsolation.setup``'s post-mkdtemp block in\n  a future wiring task.\n* :func:`superclaude.cli.install_hooks.install_hooks` — the underlying\n  installer this adapter wraps.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom ..install_hooks import install_hooks\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"HookDeployFailed\",\n    \"deploy_hooks_to\",\n]\n\n\n# Relative paths under the per-eval HOME that the adapter writes to.\n_SETTINGS_RELPATH = (\".claude\", \"settings.json\")\n_HOOKS_JSON_RELPATH = (\".claude\", \"hooks.json\")\n\n\nclass HookDeployFailed(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised by :func:`deploy_hooks_to` when hook deployment cannot complete.\n\n    Carries a short kebab-case :attr:`error_tag` the orchestrator\n    (:class:`EvalRunner`, T03.x) routes into\n    ``EvalRunner.outcome.artifacts`` so failed evals can be bucketed\n    without parsing the human-readable message.\n\n    Attributes:\n        error_tag: Short identifier for the failure mode. One of:\n\n            * ``\"refused-real-home\"`` — ``home_path`` resolved to (or\n              under) the real ``~/.claude/``; defense-in-depth guard\n              fired BEFORE any filesystem write.\n            * ``\"install-hooks-failed\"`` — the underlying\n              :func:`install_hooks` returned ``success=False``. The\n              installer's message is preserved in :attr:`detail`.\n            * ``\"hooks-json-copy-failed\"`` — the verbatim\n              ``hooks.json`` copy raised :class:`OSError`. The OS\n              error message is preserved in :attr:`detail`.\n            * ``\"source-hooks-json-missing\"`` — the source\n              ``src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json`` is not on disk.\n              Should never happen in a working checkout; surfaced\n              explicitly to make missing-source bugs loud.\n\n        home_path: The per-eval HOME the adapter was asked to deploy to.\n        detail: Human-readable failure detail (installer message,\n            ``OSError`` rendering, etc.). Never contains the error_tag\n            itself so the two surfaces stay independent.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        *,\n        error_tag: str,\n        home_path: Path,\n        detail: str,\n    ) -> None:\n        self.error_tag = error_tag\n        self.home_path = home_path\n        self.detail = detail\n        super().__init__(\n            f\"COMP-014 hook deploy failed [{error_tag}] for home_path={home_path!s}: {detail}\"\n        )\n\n\ndef _real_user_claude_dir() -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Resolve the user's real ``~/.claude/`` directory.\n\n    Returns the resolved (symlinks collapsed) path. Used by the\n    defense-in-depth refusal so a per-eval ``home_path`` that is a\n    symlink to the real user HOME still fails the equality / descendant\n    check.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    return (Path.home() / \".claude\").resolve(strict=False)\n\n\ndef _refuse_real_home(home_path: Path) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Reject ``home_path`` when any adapter write would land under real ``~/.claude/``.\n\n    Two attack surfaces are caught:\n\n    * ``home_path`` itself resolves to (or under) the real\n      ``~/.claude/`` directory — every adapter write is anchored at\n      ``home_path``, so this catches direct attempts.\n    * ``home_path / .claude`` resolves to (or under) the real\n      ``~/.claude/`` — the subtle case where the caller passes their\n      own ``$HOME`` as ``home_path`` and the adapter's\n      ``<home_path>/.claude/settings.json`` write would otherwise\n      silently target the real configuration directory.\n\n    Both sides are resolved with ``strict=False`` so a symlink in either\n    direction is collapsed before comparison.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    real = _real_user_claude_dir()\n    resolved_home = home_path.resolve(strict=False)\n    resolved_claude_subdir = (home_path / \".claude\").resolve(strict=False)\n\n    def _matches(candidate: Path) -> bool:\n        return candidate == real or candidate.is_relative_to(real)\n\n    if _matches(resolved_home) or _matches(resolved_claude_subdir):\n        raise HookDeployFailed(\n            error_tag=\"refused-real-home\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            detail=(\n                f\"home_path {home_path!s} (resolved={resolved_home!s}; \"\n                f\"claude_subdir={resolved_claude_subdir!s}) intersects the \"\n                f\"real user Claude dir {real!s}; refusing to write hooks \"\n                f\"under the real ~/.claude/\"\n            ),\n        )\n\n\ndef _source_hooks_json() -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Locate the canonical ``src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json``.\n\n    Mirrors :func:`superclaude.cli.install_hooks._get_hooks_source` so\n    the adapter and the installer agree on which file is the source of\n    truth. Imported via the public ``install_hooks`` to avoid coupling\n    on a private helper that may move.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # ``hook_adapter.py`` lives at src/superclaude/cli/eval/hook_adapter.py.\n    # Two parents up is the package root (src/superclaude/).\n    package_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent\n    return package_root / \"hooks\" / \"hooks.json\"\n\n\ndef deploy_hooks_to(home_path: Path) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Deploy SuperClaude hooks into the per-eval HOME at ``home_path``.\n\n    Two filesystem effects, in this order:\n\n    1. :func:`install_hooks` is invoked with ``target_path`` resolved to\n       ``<home_path>/.claude/settings.json``. This copies the hook\n       scripts to ``<home_path>/.claude/hooks/`` and merges hook\n       registrations into a fresh ``<home_path>/.claude/settings.json``.\n    2. The source ``src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json`` is copied\n       verbatim to ``<home_path>/.claude/hooks.json`` (byte-identical\n       via :func:`shutil.copy2`).\n\n    Idempotent: re-invocation on the same ``home_path`` produces\n    identical filesystem state. The installer skips already-deployed\n    scripts and matcher-colliding registrations; the verbatim copy\n    overwrites with the same bytes when the source is unchanged.\n\n    Args:\n        home_path: Per-eval HOME directory created by\n            :class:`HomeIsolation.setup`. MUST NOT resolve to the real\n            ``~/.claude/`` directory — the pre-flight guard rejects\n            that case before any write happens.\n\n    Raises:\n        HookDeployFailed: Any of the failure modes documented on\n            :class:`HookDeployFailed.error_tag`. Each failure mode\n            carries enough detail (``home_path`` + ``detail``) for the\n            orchestrator to bucket the eval and surface the underlying\n            cause in operator-facing reports.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Step 0 — defense-in-depth refusal. Must run BEFORE any write so\n    # `install_hooks` never even materializes a directory under real\n    # `~/.claude/`.\n    _refuse_real_home(home_path)\n\n    source_hooks_json = _source_hooks_json()\n    if not source_hooks_json.exists():\n        raise HookDeployFailed(\n            error_tag=\"source-hooks-json-missing\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            detail=f\"source hooks.json not found at {source_hooks_json!s}\",\n        )\n\n    # Step 1 — delegate scripts + settings.json merge to install_hooks.\n    settings_path = home_path.joinpath(*_SETTINGS_RELPATH)\n    success, message = install_hooks(target_path=settings_path, force=False)\n    if not success:\n        raise HookDeployFailed(\n            error_tag=\"install-hooks-failed\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            detail=message,\n        )\n\n    # Step 2 — verbatim hooks.json copy. Required by the AC: the\n    # per-eval HOME must carry a SHA256-identical copy of the source\n    # registrations so post-mortem inspection / FR-G* gate tests can\n    # diff against the canonical file.\n    dest_hooks_json = home_path.joinpath(*_HOOKS_JSON_RELPATH)\n    try:\n        dest_hooks_json.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        shutil.copy2(source_hooks_json, dest_hooks_json)\n    except OSError as exc:\n        raise HookDeployFailed(\n            error_tag=\"hooks-json-copy-failed\",\n            home_path=home_path,\n            detail=f\"verbatim hooks.json copy from {source_hooks_json!s} \"\n            f\"to {dest_hooks_json!s} failed: {exc}\",\n        ) from exc\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":238,"oldLines":11,"newStart":238,"newLines":14,"lines":["     # Step 2 — verbatim hooks.json copy. Pin a\nsingle integration site at\n`src/superclaude/cli/eval/hook_adapter.py` so the cliEval harness and\nthe production user-install path stay byte-for-byte aligned.\n\n## Adapter contract\n\n### Signature\n\n```python\ndef deploy_hooks_to(home_path: Path) -> None: ...\n```\n\n`home_path` is the per-eval HOME directory `HomeIsolation.setup`\nmaterialized (i.e., `<scratch_root>/<eval_id>-XXXXXX/`). The adapter\nreturns `None` on success and raises `HookDeployFailed` on any failure.\n\n### Side effects (in order)\n\n1. **Defense-in-depth refusal.** Resolve both `home_path` and\n   `home_path / .claude` with `strict=False` and reject if either\n   intersects the real `~/.claude/` directory (equality OR descendant).\n   This catches: (a) `home_path` symlinked to real HOME, (b) callers\n   passing their `$HOME` as `home_path` (which would direct the\n   `<home>/.claude/settings.json` write straight into real `~/.claude/`).\n2. **`install_hooks` delegation.** Invoke\n   `install_hooks(target_path=home_path / \".claude\" / \"settings.json\",\n   force=False)`. The installer copies hook scripts to\n   `<home>/.claude/hooks/` and merges hook registrations into\n   `<home>/.claude/settings.json`.\n3. **Verbatim `hooks.json` copy.** Copy\n   `src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json` byte-for-byte to\n   `<home>/.claude/hooks.json` via the dedicated\n   `_copy_hooks_json_verbatim` helper (a thin wrapper over\n   `shutil.copy2` whose only purpose is to be a clean monkeypatch seam\n   for tests).\n\n### Idempotency\n\nRe-invocation on the same `home_path` produces identical filesystem\nstate. `install_hooks` is itself idempotent (existing scripts are\nskipped without `--force`; the settings.json merge skips matcher\ncollisions). The verbatim copy uses `shutil.copy2` which overwrites\nwith identical bytes when the source is unchanged.\n\n### Error contract — `HookDeployFailed.error_tag`\n\nThe exception carries a structured `error_tag` so `EvalRunner`\n(T03.x) can route the failure into `EvalRunner.outcome.artifacts`\nwithout parsing human-readable strings.\n\n| `error_tag`                  | Triggered when                                                                 |\n|------------------------------|--------------------------------------------------------------------------------|\n| `refused-real-home`          | `home_path` or `home_path/.claude` resolves to (or under) real `~/.claude/`.   |\n| `source-hooks-json-missing`  | `src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json` is absent on disk (broken checkout).        |\n| `install-hooks-failed`       | `install_hooks` returned `success=False`. Installer message in `detail`.       |\n| `hooks-json-copy-failed`     | `_copy_hooks_json_verbatim` raised `OSError`. OS error in `detail`.            |\n\nAll four tags are lowercase kebab-case with alphanumeric tokens — the\ntest `test_error_tags_are_kebab_case_strings` pins this shape so the\norchestrator routing table cannot drift.\n\n## Acceptance criteria mapping (T02.14)\n\n| AC bullet                                                                                                          | Evidence                                                                                                        |\n|---|---|\n| `deploy_hooks_to(home_path)` exists in `src/superclaude/cli/eval/hook_adapter.py` and calls `install_hooks`         | `test_deploy_hooks_to_writes_settings_json_under_home_path`, `test_settings_json_merge_uses_per_eval_target_path` |\n| Adapter raises `HookDeployFailed` with `error_tag` on `install_hooks` failure                                       | `test_install_hooks_failure_propagates_as_hook_deploy_failed`, `test_error_tags_are_kebab_case_strings`           |\n| Re-invocation produces identical FS state (idempotency)                                                            | `test_re_invocation_is_idempotent`                                                                              |\n| `<home>/.claude/hooks.json` is byte-identical to `src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json` (SHA256 assertion)              | `test_hooks_json_is_byte_identical_to_source`                                                                   |\n| Adapter never writes to real `~/.claude/` (mtime fixture)                                                          | `test_real_user_claude_dir_is_untouched`, `test_refuses_when_home_path_is_real_user_claude_dir`, `test_refuses_when_home_path_is_descendant_of_real_user_claude_dir` |\n| Adapter contract documented (this spec)                                                                            | This file                                                                                                       |\n\n## Verbatim hooks.json copy — why a separate step?\n\n`install_hooks` was designed for the production user-install path:\nits job is to copy hook scripts and additively merge hook\nregistrations into the user's `settings.json`. It never copies the\nsource `hooks.json` to the destination because the source-of-truth\nregistrations are already represented inside the merged\n`settings.json`.\n\nThe cliEval harness needs a second guarantee: the per-eval HOME\nmust carry a byte-identical copy of `hooks.json` so:\n\n* Post-mortem inspection can SHA256-compare the source against what\n  the per-eval HOME shipped without re-deriving registrations from\n  the merged `settings.json`.\n* The FR-G* capability-gate tests can diff the deployed file against\n  the canonical source to detect any unexpected divergence in the\n  per-eval surface.\n\nRouting this copy through the dedicated `_copy_hooks_json_verbatim`\nhelper isolates the seam from `install_hooks`'s own `shutil.copy2`\nusage (it copies hook scripts via the same API). Tests that simulate\nan `OSError` on the verbatim copy patch this helper rather than the\nshared `shutil.copy2` global so they don't accidentally also break\nthe installer's script-copy pipeline.\n\n## Defense-in-depth — refusing real `~/.claude/`\n\nThe refusal must catch two distinct attack surfaces:\n\n1. **Direct.** `home_path` itself resolves to (or under) real\n   `~/.claude/`. Catches symlink shenanigans.\n2. **Subdirectory leak.** `home_path / .claude` resolves to (or\n   under) real `~/.claude/`. Catches the case where the caller\n   passes their `$HOME` as `home_path` — every adapter write under\n   `<home_path>/.claude/` would land on the real configuration\n   directory.\n\nBoth sides are resolved with `strict=False` (the real directory may\nexist, may not; the per-eval HOME may not have a `.claude/`\nsubdirectory yet) and compared with `==` OR `is_relative_to`. Tests\npatch `Path.home` so the assertion never has to touch the operator's\nactual `$HOME`.\n\n## Files\n\n* `src/superclaude/cli/eval/hook_adapter.py` — adapter module.\n* `src/superclaude/cli/eval/__init__.py` — re-exports\n  `HookDeployFailed`, `deploy_hooks_to`.\n* `tests/cli/eval/test_hook_adapter.py` — 12 tests pinning the\n  acceptance criteria above.\n\n## Verification\n\n* `uv run pytest tests/cli/eval/test_hook_adapter.py -v` → 12 passed.\n* `uv run pytest tests/cli/test_install_hooks.py -v` → 8 failures\n  pre-existing on the branch (the `reject-workspace-writes.sh`\n  fixture is absent in the fake source-tree builder used by those\n  tests; the adapter does NOT regress them — same 8 fail on a clean\n  checkout). {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01Xq1cqwW9BcHAWEH7V3dEp7","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Bat4JKERpvrP4eu1Ammqhg","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/config/workspace/IronClaude/.dev/releases/current/cliEval/artifacts/D-0034/notes.md","content":"# D-0034 — Notes (Task T02.14)\n\n## Design decisions\n\n### Why a verbatim copy on top of `install_hooks`?\n\n`install_hooks` merges hook registrations into `settings.json` but\ndoes not copy `hooks.json` itself to the destination. The T02.14 AC\nrequires `<home_path>/.claude/hooks.json` to be byte-identical\n(SHA256-equal) to `src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json` for two reasons:\n\n1. The per-eval HOME becomes self-describing: a post-mortem operator\n   can diff the file against the source to confirm the deployed\n   registrations match the source-of-truth.\n2. FR-G* gate tests can read the deployed file directly without\n   un-merging it from `settings.json`.\n\nA separate step `shutil.copy2` (via the dedicated\n`_copy_hooks_json_verbatim` helper) preserves the source mtime and\nmetadata, which gives idempotency: re-running the adapter produces\nthe same destination bytes AND the same mtime, so consumers that\nmtime-watch the file see no change.\n\n### Why a private `_copy_hooks_json_verbatim` helper?\n\n`install_hooks` uses `shutil.copy2` internally to copy hook scripts.\nThe adapter's verbatim-copy step also uses `shutil.copy2`. If the\ntest for `hooks-json-copy-failed` patched the shared `shutil.copy2`\nglobal, it would also break `install_hooks`'s script-copy pipeline\nand the patched OSError would surface from the installer first,\nmaking the test verify the wrong failure mode.\n\nThe helper is the smallest possible seam: a one-line wrapper whose\nonly purpose is to be monkeypatch-friendly. The test patches\n`superclaude.cli.eval.hook_adapter._copy_hooks_json_verbatim`\ndirectly, leaving `shutil.copy2` untouched for the installer to use.\n\n### Why resolve `home_path / .claude` in the refusal guard?\n\nThe naive guard \"refuse if `home_path` resolves to real `~/.claude/`\"\nmisses the case where the caller passes their own `$HOME` as\n`home_path`. The adapter writes `<home_path>/.claude/settings.json`,\nwhich would land directly on `~/.claude/settings.json` if\n`home_path == $HOME`. The fix is to additionally resolve\n`home_path / .claude` and check it against the real Claude dir.\n\nBoth checks are necessary:\n- `home_path` direct check catches `home_path == ~/.claude/` and\n  symlinks pointing there.\n- `home_path / .claude` check catches `home_path == $HOME` and any\n  path whose `.claude` subdir would land under real `~/.claude/`.\n\n### Source resolution\n\nThe adapter resolves\n`src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json` independently of\n`install_hooks._get_hooks_source` to avoid coupling on a private\nhelper. The package layout convention is stable:\n`hook_adapter.py` lives at `src/superclaude/cli/eval/hook_adapter.py`,\nso `__file__.resolve().parent.parent.parent` is the package root.\n\n## Failure-mode taxonomy\n\nThe `error_tag` taxonomy is deliberately small (4 tags) and stable so\nthe EvalRunner's routing table can hard-code them. All tags are\nlowercase kebab-case with alphanumeric tokens. Adding new tags is\nallowed; renaming existing tags is not (the orchestrator's bucket\nmapping treats them as a public API surface).\n\n| Tag                          | Where raised                              |\n|------------------------------|-------------------------------------------|\n| `refused-real-home`          | `_refuse_real_home`                       |\n| `source-hooks-json-missing`  | Early existence check in `deploy_hooks_to` |\n| `install-hooks-failed`       | After `install_hooks` returns `(False, …)` |\n| `hooks-json-copy-failed`     | After `_copy_hooks_json_verbatim` raises   |\n\n## Pre-existing test failures (out of scope)\n\n`tests/cli/test_install_hooks.py` has 8 pre-existing failures on the\nbranch tip caused by the test's fake source-tree fixture missing\n`reject-workspace-writes.sh`. These failures predate T02.14 and are\nnot caused by the adapter — verified by `git stash && pytest && git\nstash pop` showing the same 8 failures on a clean checkout. Cleanup\ntracked separately; out of scope for this task.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3177,"cache_read_input_tokens":149590,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3177,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":54,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available","speed":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9b082b2d-581e-4312-b64d-a1e7c7afbf63","uuid":"91195e24-2a9c-4bfc-9240-d48c5edad097"}
